Conclusion: SenWorld 提供了一条隐私安全、可复现且经过分布检验的评估数据生成路径，其标签由构造本身固定，避免了传统标注方法的主观性和隐私风险。

Abstract: Smartphone personal assistants reason over longitudinal personal data, yet evaluating them requires context-rich evaluation data whose correct answers are known, and real device traces are too privacy-sensitive to share. To address this challenge, we present SenWorld, a physically grounded, deterministic, event-sourced digital-twin simulation that generates such data with ground truth fixed by construction. In SenWorld, personas live through a full day in a world built from real map, weather, holiday, and network data; every observable signal is archived in full-system snapshots; and each evaluation case is labeled by a pointer to an existing record rather than by post-hoc annotation or a large language model (LLM) judge. We evaluate this method with 16 personas in Beijing. The generated data closely matches the held-out real-user benchmark in category distribution (Jensen--Shannon divergence (JSD) 0.070) and in the daily rhythm of communication records (JSD below 0.1), though generated records remain shorter than real ones. Without scripted interaction, personas form a fully reciprocated dialogue subgraph and differentiated behavioral repertoires. Projected into 717 evaluation cases, the generated data exposes 78 failures in a production smartphone assistant, concentrating on call and Short Message Service (SMS) records while contacts, schedules, and alarms never fail. The snapshot pointer confirms each failure as an assistant-side retrieval error, with no LLM judge involved. Overall, SenWorld offers a privacy-safe, reproducible, and distribution-checked path to evaluation data whose labels are fixed by construction.

</details>


### [39] [EvoThink: Evolving Thinking in Large Reasoning Models via Self-Pruning and Aha-Moment Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19962)
*Xinbang Dai,Zheyu Xin,Huikang Hu,Lin Ren,Rihui Jin,Guohui Xiao,Guilin Qi,Kuicai Dong,Zhaocheng Du,Yuyang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EvoThink是一个通过自剪枝训练（SPT）和“啊哈时刻”偏好优化（AMPO）来减少大推理模型（LRM）过度思考冗余验证、同时提升推理效率与能力的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LRM常因冗余验证步骤而过度思考，现有方法（如快慢思考切换、推理轨迹压缩）无法细粒度区分有益与冗余步骤，可能导致在追求效率时损害推理能力。

Method: EvoThink由两部分组成：1) 自剪枝训练（SPT），一种无监督方法，迭代剪枝冗余推理步骤并在精简轨迹上自训练；2) “啊哈时刻”偏好优化（AMPO），受遗传算法启发，识别有价值的失败推理尝试，合成从错到对的“啊哈时刻”数据，优化模型内化该推理模式。

Result: 在数学推理和代码生成基准上的广泛评估表明，EvoThink不仅显著减少了推理时的token使用量，还提升了LRM的推理能力。

Conclusion: EvoThink能够同时提升推理效率与推理能力，通过剪枝冗余步骤和利用失败尝试中的有价值信息实现协同优化。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) often suffer from overthinking due to redundant verification steps. Existing approaches for mitigating overthinking, such as fast-slow thinking switching and reasoning trajectory compression, fail to make a fine-grained distinction between beneficial and redundant steps within the LRM's reasoning process, and may thus impair reasoning capability in their pursuit of efficiency. To simultaneously improve reasoning efficiency and capability, we propose EvoThink, a framework that reduces redundant verification and encourages the exploration of new reasoning paths. EvoThink comprises two key components: Self-Pruning Training (SPT), an unsupervised method that iteratively prunes redundant reasoning steps and self-trains on the concise trajectories; and Aha-Moment Preference Optimization (AMPO), which, inspired by genetic algorithms, identifies valuable failed reasoning attempts, synthesizes from-wrong-to-right aha-moment data, and optimizes the model to internalize this reasoning pattern. Extensive evaluations across mathematical reasoning and code generation benchmarks demonstrate that EvoThink not only substantially reduces inference-time token usage but also improves the reasoning capability of LRMs.

</details>


### [40] [The Giant Hippocampus: From Structural Monoculture to a System of Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19973)
*Jaeho Seol*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出当前AI领域普遍采用单一Transformer架构扩展至所有任务的做法存在结构性错误，而神经科学表明不同认知功能依赖不同的神经结构。作者提出应借鉴脑细胞架构学的证据，构建异构拓扑网络——即由具有不同归纳偏置的独立模块通过标准化接口组成的系统，在训练前即明确模块化设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI研究将Transformer作为通用架构应用于文本、图像、语音等所有任务，但神经科学证据表明大脑皮层是异质的——不同区域具有不同的细胞结构和功能特化。这种"一个架构适用所有"的做法与神经科学的发现存在根本性矛盾，作者认为这种差距是可测量的结构错误，而非单纯的风格差异。

Method: 论文采用功能性分析方法，追踪了从Brodmann细胞架构学到单细胞Patch-seq的百年神经科学证据，对比了CNN（其局部感受野和层次深度编码了正确的归纳偏置）与Transformer的架构演进。分析了"硬件彩票"如何使Transformer成为阻力最小的路径而非原则性选择，以及MoE中专家同质化的问题。最终将Transformer定位为海马体的功能类似物，而非通用皮层模型。

Result: 论文揭示当前AI领域将Transformer（本质是海马体的功能类似物）应用于它从未为设计过的任务（听觉、执行门控、工作记忆等），相当于把整个皮层当作一个巨大的布罗卡区或海马体来使用。这种标准化的"巨型海马体"范式在结构层面存在根本性缺陷。

Conclusion: 作者提出异构拓扑网络（Heterogeneous Topological Network）作为替代方案——一个"系统的系统"，其中不同模块保持其计算所需的独立归纳偏置，通过标准化接口通信。这是一种训练前的设计规范：以结构证据作为设计输入来指定模块化，而非从训练后模型的行为中逆向工程架构。

Abstract: AI researchers describe state-of-the-art models as one thing repeated at scale: the Transformer, wired identically for text, pixels, or speech. Neuroscientists describe the cortex as a mosaic - dense Layer 4 in visual cortex for spatial encoding, thick Layers 5/6 in motion cortex for temporal integration - different jobs solved by different structures. This paper argues the gap is a structural error, not a stylistic one, and is measurable. A century of cytoarchitecture, from Brodmann to single-cell Patch-seq, shows distinct cognitive functions are implemented by qualitatively different structures, not by rescaling one template. The convolutional neural network is the field's own proof: local receptive fields and hierarchical depth encoded this prior directly, reaching strong image recognition on far less data than later architectures needed. The paper traces how this lesson was discarded: the "Hardware Lottery" made the Transformer the path of least resistance, not the principled choice, and Mixture-of-Experts, often cited as diversity, in fact partitions parameters among identical experts. A functionalist analysis shows the Transformer is best understood as a functional analog of the hippocampal formation, not a general-purpose cortex - the same mistake as treating cortex as one giant Broca's area, except the field has now standardized on a giant hippocampus, applied to tasks it was never built for: audition, executive gating, working memory. The paper closes with an alternative: a Heterogeneous Topological Network, a System of Systems in which distinct modules keep the inductive bias their computation demands and communicate through standardized interfaces. This is a design discipline for AI architects, not cognitive science: specify modularity before training, using structural evidence as a design input rather than reverse-engineering architecture from a trained model's behavior.

</details>


### [41] [Coordinating from Memory: Graph-Structured Experience Reuse for Multi-Agent Adaptation in Dynamic Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19985)
*Chengxiao Dai,Zhanhui Lin,Zhaokun Yan,Youyang Ni,Chenjun Lei,Luyan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出图结构经验记忆框架，通过编码历史协调经验为异构图，利用图神经网络检索相似经验来指导多智能体在动态制造环境中的协调，在动态柔性作业车间调度基准上使最大完工时间减少4.1%-10.0%，适应时间减少33%-38%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MARL方法在动态制造环境中将每个扰动事件独立处理，丢弃了宝贵的协调经验，无法加速未来的适应过程。

Method: 提出GSEM框架，将历史协调经验编码为异构关系图（包含任务依赖、机器状态、智能体协作模式），当新扰动发生时，用图神经网络检索结构相似的历史经验，指导策略适应而非从头学习。

Result: 在三种扰动类型的动态柔性作业车间调度基准上，GSEM最大完工时间减少4.1%-10.0%，适应时间减少33%-38%，且优势在高扰动频率下更明显。

Conclusion: 图结构编码和基于相似性的检索是必要的，学到的协调模式具有跨扰动泛化能力。

Abstract: Dynamic manufacturing environments require multi-agent systems to coordinate effectively under frequent operational disturbances such as machine failures, urgent job arrivals, and processing time variations. Existing multi-agent reinforcement learning approaches treat each disturbance episode independently, discarding valuable coordination experience that could accelerate future adaptation. In this paper, we propose a Graph-Structured Experiential Memory (GSEM) framework for multi-agent coordination in dynamic manufacturing. The framework encodes historical coordination episodes as heterogeneous relational graphs that capture task dependencies, machine states, and inter-agent collaboration patterns. When a new disturbance occurs, a graph neural network-based retrieval mechanism identifies structurally similar past episodes, enabling experience-guided policy adaptation rather than learning from scratch. Abstract: Machine Learning models are widely used for automating classification tasks by extracting statistical patterns from data. However, their performance deteriorates if the data distribution changes, making them ill-suited to handle uncertain and evolving information. Moreover, they provide limited support for integrating prior knowledge. To address these limitations, we present CLARK (Closed-loop Learning for Adaptive Reasoning over Knowledge Graphs), a framework that integrates knowledge graphs, symbolic rule mining, and probabilistic reasoning under the Logic Programs with Markov Logic Networks (LP$^{\text{MLN}}$) formalism. Starting from CACTUS-derived KGs, CLARK translates graph structure into an LP$^{\text{MLN}}$ program and iteratively enriches it with candidate rules proposed by symbolic learners. These rules are calibrated through probabilistic weight learning, enabling reasoning under uncertainty and refinement of the underlying graph structure. We evaluate CLARK on two medical datasets, analysing both rule quality and downstream classification performance. Results demonstrate that CLARK leads to improved classification performance and more generalisable inference. Overall, CLARK provides a principled approach to constructing adaptive, interpretable, knowledge-driven models for classification.

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种风险约束的自动化运维修复框架，将安全修复重新定义为CMDP问题，通过三维风险分解（爆炸半径、可逆性、认知不确定性）和自适应人工干预门控机制，在降低39%误修复率的同时提升2.5个百分点的修复成功率，并减少17%的升级工单量。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: (i) 将安全修复重构为风险约束干预决策问题，建模为约束马尔可夫决策过程(CMDP)，在有限误修复率(FRR)约束下最大化修复成功率；(ii) 提出三维风险分解框架（爆炸半径、可逆性、认知不确定性），提供可解释的逐操作安全接口；(iii) 设计上下文自适应的人工介入门控机制(HITL)，根据工单负载和业务重要性动态调整升级策略；策略通过历史工单日志离线学习。

Abstract: In modern IT operations (IT-Ops), the cost of an incorrect repair often exceeds the cost of no action at all. Yet existing automated remediation systems are designed to generate actions rather than to decide whether intervention is warranted, leaving safety as an afterthought enforced by manual approval. This paper makes three contributions to close this gap: (i) we reformulate safe remediation as a risk-constrained intervention decision problem and cast it as a Constrained Markov Decision Process (CMDP), in which the agent maximizes repair success subject to a bounded false remediation rate (FRR); (ii) we introduce a three-dimensional risk decomposition comprising blast radius, reversibility, and epistemic uncertainty, providing operators with an interpretable per-action safety interface; and (iii) we design a context-adaptive human-in-the-loop (HITL) gate that turns escalation from a binary failsafe into a bandwidth-aware control layer responsive to on-call load and business criticality. The full policy is learned offline from historical incident logs, enabling explicit control of the expected FRR. Experiments on the Train Ticket microservice benchmark with Chaos Mesh fault injection and an RCAEval-aligned fault taxonomy show that our framework reduces FRR by 39% while improving repair success by 2.5 points over a strong runbook baseline, and reduces on-call escalation load by 17% relative to a fixed-threshold variant.

</details>

*Bing-Yue Wu,Chia-Tung Ho,Haoyu Yang,Brucek Khailany,Vidya A. Chhabria*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EvoDRC是一个用于自动修复版图设计规则违例的技能进化框架，通过在目标设计中持续收集可追溯的修复经验来进化和更新修复技能，在DAC26 DRC基准测试的七个模块级设计上实现了73.5%的违例减少率。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: EvoDRC首先利用从无关参考设计中蒸馏的知识初始化各层修复技能；然后将版图分解为有界修复区域，并为每个区域分配一个LLM修复智能体；使用局部DRC分析、连通性检查和影响预览工具对提议修改提供反馈；最后将修复操作及其DRV变化存储在知识数据库中，用于持续进化修复技能。

Result: 在DAC26 DRC基准测试的七个模块级设计上，EvoDRC相较于报告基线实现了73.5%的整体违例减少率。

Conclusion: EvoDRC通过技能进化框架有效实现了模块级DRC修复的自动化，验证了利用跨设计知识蒸馏和在线经验收集来持续优化修复策略的可行性。

Abstract: Design rule check (DRC) closure remains a major bottleneck in advanced-node physical design. Although detailed routers are rule-aware, residual design rule violations (DRVs) often require manual engineering change order iterations. Automating this process is challenging because repairs must account for complex geometric interactions, preserve circuit connectivity, and avoid introducing new violations. We present EvoDRC, a skill-evolution framework for agentic block-level DRC repair. EvoDRC initializes layer-specific repair skills using knowledge distilled from an unrelated reference design and continuously evolves these skills using traceable repair experience collected from the target design. EvoDRC decomposes the layout into bounded repair regions and assigns an LLM repair agent to each region. Local DRC analysis, connectivity-checking, and impact-preview tools provide feedback on proposed modifications. Repair operations and their resulting DRV changes are stored in a knowledge database and used to evolve the repair skills. Experiments on seven block-level designs from the DAC26 DRC Benchmark show that EvoDRC achieves a 73.5\% overall reduction compared to the reported baseline.

</details>


### [45] [Global Difference Constraint Propagation for Constraint Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20022)
*Lucas Kletzander,Jip J. Dekker,Andreas Schutt,Peter J. Stuckey*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了差异约束的全局传播器，通过同时处理所有差异约束而非单独传播，并利用惰性子句生成求解器中的解释机制，显著提升了传播效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的有限域传播算法将每个差异约束视为独立的传播器，虽然保证了求解的完备性，但效率低下。本文旨在构建一个全局传播器，同时处理所有差异约束，以提高传播速度。

Method: 基于SAT模理论求解器中的差异约束理论求解器，构建边界一致的全局传播器。重点在于适配传播器的特殊需求，特别是如何为惰性子句生成求解器生成传播的解释。

Result: 实验表明，全局处理差异约束相比标准传播方法有显著性能提升。

Conclusion: 全局差异约束传播器能够有效替代单独传播策略，在保证求解完备性的同时大幅提高效率，尤其适用于惰性子句生成框架。

Abstract: Difference constraints of the form $x - y \leq d$ are well studied, with efficient algorithms for satisfaction and implication, because of their connection to shortest paths. Finite domain propagation algorithms, however, typically do not make use of these algorithms, and treat each difference constraint as a separate propagator. Propagation does guarantee completeness of solving, but can be needlessly slow. In this paper we describe how to build a (bounds consistent) global propagator for difference constraints that treats them all simultaneously. SAT modulo theory solvers have included theory solvers for difference constraints for some time. While a theory solver for difference constraints gives the basis of a global difference constraint propagator, we show how the requirements on the propagator are quite different. Crucially, we show how to explain propagations by a global difference constraint propagator, in order to use it within a lazy clause generation solver. We give experiments showing that treating difference constraints globally can substantially improve on the standard propagation approach.

</details>


### [46] [Reading and Steering Representations of Materials-Science Mechanisms in an Open-Weight Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20058)
*Markus J. Buehler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过三种实验分离形式（隐藏状态概念可读性、状态变换的定向控制、因果干预）证明google/gemma-4-E4B-it模型内部编码了材料科学物理机制信息，且物理关系在受控状态变化中比在绝对状态中更明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型能正确回答科学问题，但正确输出无法揭示模型是否真正表征或应用了底层物理规律，需要探究模型内部是否编码了可分离的物理机制信息。

Method: 结合匹配的直接和Jacobian词汇读出、无选项状态几何、60条物理定律的反事实基准和因果干预方法，通过50个材料描述和72提示基准进行实验，包括逆向物理输入状态变换比较和双向因果干预。

Result: Jacobian透镜复现概念排名，盲识别9/10机制族；物理反向输入状态变换正确定向39/40方向性定律（词汇对照接近随机）；双向干预在所有12个匹配案例中改变答案概率，反事实状态补丁跨机制和答案格式传递对立决策信号。

Conclusion: 物理关系在受控状态变化中比在绝对状态中更可观测，模型内部存在可分离的物理机制编码形式。

Abstract: Large language models can answer scientific questions, yet a correct output does not reveal whether the model represents or uses the governing physics. Here we show that materials science mechanism information in the open-weight google/gemma-4-E4B-it model has three experimentally separable forms: concepts are readable in individual hidden states, constitutive orientation is carried by controlled transformations between states, and selected internal representations causally control engineering answers. We combine matched direct and Jacobian vocabulary readouts, option-free state geometry, a 60-law counterfactual benchmark and causal interventions. In 50 held-out materials descriptions, three independently fitted Jacobian lenses reproduced concept ranks, and target-free word sets from both readouts enabled blinded identification of 9 of 10 mechanism families. A separate 72-prompt benchmark produced mechanism-specific hidden-state neighborhoods, but an exact graph audit showed that this apparent physical organization was equally explained by numerical comparison. We therefore compared otherwise identical prompts in which only the direction of the physical input was reversed, asking whether the resulting hidden-state movement followed the supplied constitutive law. These state transformations ordered direct, physically neutral and inverse laws across 60 frozen relations and correctly oriented 39 of 40 directional laws, whereas lexical controls were near chance. Bidirectional interventions shifted answer probabilities toward or away from the physically appropriate outcome across all 12 matched cases, while counterfactual state patches transferred opposing decision signals across mechanisms and answer formats. Physical relationships were therefore more visible in controlled state changes than in absolute states alone.

</details>


### [47] [PRO-LONG: Programmatic Memory Enables Long-Horizon Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20064)
*Alexis Fox,Junlin Wang,Paul Rosu,Bhuwan Dhingra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PRO-LONG是一个针对长时域任务LLM智能体的上下文管理框架，通过程序化记忆（完整结构化的交互日志+编码智能体搜索）来解决信息保存与检索之间的权衡。在ARC-AGI-3基准上，相比基础编码智能体平均提升18.0个百分点，最高达到76.1% pass@1，同时减少4.2-5.8倍token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时域任务需要持续的感知、推理和探索，LLM智能体在持续学习基准（如ARC-AGI-3）上表现有限。现有上下文管理方法面临信息保存与检索的显著权衡：保留更多信息会使检索相关细节变得困难。

Method: PRO-LONG框架的核心是程序化记忆：维护一个完整的、结构化的交互日志，并利用编码智能体（coding agents）的最新进展来高效搜索历史记录。这是一种最小化的上下文管理框架，专门针对长时域、探索性场景设计。

Result: 在完整ARC-AGI-3公开游戏集上，PRO-LONG在多个前沿模型上平均提升18.0个百分点（相比基础编码智能体），最高达到76.1% pass@1，匹配或超过最先进专用框架，同时节省4.2-5.8倍token。在Fable 5上以总成本$1,750实现97.4% best@2。

Conclusion: 提出了一种简单有效的上下文管理策略，通过结构化日志+编码智能体搜索打破了信息保存与检索的权衡，显著提升了LLM智能体在长时域任务上的表现，同时大幅降低了计算成本。

Abstract: Long-horizon tasks require sustained perception, reasoning, and exploration, and are a persistent challenge for large language model (LLM) agents. This gap is reflected in their limited performance on continual learning benchmarks such as ARC-AGI-3, especially when models are evaluated out of the box. Various agent harnesses have been proposed to close this gap, and each commits to a strategy for handling long sequences of observations, i.e., what information to save from the environment and how to load it into model context, a choice we argue is particularly consequential. Existing methods for context management face a significant tradeoff, as preserving more information makes retrieving relevant details less tractable. We propose PRO-LONG, a minimal context management framework built around programmatic memory for LLM agents in long-horizon, exploratory settings. PRO-LONG addresses the tradeoff by keeping a complete, structured interaction log and capitalizing on recent progress in coding agents to search this history efficiently. On the full ARC-AGI-3 public game set, PRO-LONG improves over a base coding agent by an average of 18.0 percentage points across frontier models, and matches or exceeds state-of-the-art specialized harnesses (up to 76.1% pass@1) while using 4.2-5.8x fewer tokens. With Fable 5, PRO-LONG achieves 97.4% best@2 at a total cost of \$1,750. Relevant code and logs are available at https://github.com/alexisfox7/PRO-LONG.

</details>


### [48] [TRUST-ESD: A Risk-Calibrated and Governance-Aware AI Framework for Enterprise Strategic Decision Support Under Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20065)
*Tian Qiu,Li Yan,Mahabubur Rahman Miraj,Shanqin Yi,Md Intekhab Rahman Galib,Jahid Hasan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TRUST-ESD，一个面向企业战略决策支持的风险校准与治理感知框架，通过预测效用估计、共形不确定性校准、CVaR下行风险评分、风险记忆检索、策略即代码治理、可解释性和人工监督等模块，在不确定性下推荐平衡价值、可靠性、风险暴露和合规性的策略。实验表明，与强基线相比，TRUST-ESD在风险调整效用、风险暴露、CVaR、校准误差、解释保真度和治理合规性方面均有显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业战略决策支持需要AI系统不仅准确，还要具备不确定性感知、风险校准、可解释性和治理合规性，现有方法仅通过最大期望效用选择行动，缺乏对风险、校准和治理的综合考量。

Method: TRUST-ESD框架包含六大核心模块：1）预测效用估计评估反事实策略；2）共形不确定性校准量化预测可靠性；3）CVaR下行风险评分衡量尾部风险；4）风险记忆检索存储历史风险案例；5）策略即代码（Policy-as-Code）治理确保合规；6）可解释性与人工监督机制。整体通过多目标优化平衡价值、可靠性、风险暴露和合规性。

Result: 相比强不确定性感知基线，TRUST-ESD在6项关键指标上取得提升：风险调整效用+7.95%，风险暴露-23.22%，CVaR-23.78%，校准误差-13.89%，解释保真度+10.90%，治理合规性+9.76%，且保持竞争性的预测精度。消融实验和案例分析验证了各模块的联合贡献。

Conclusion: 不确定性校准、下行风险评分、风险记忆、可解释性和治理验证共同提升了可信赖的企业决策质量，TRUST-ESD为企业级战略决策提供了一种兼顾准确性与可信赖性的综合解决方案。

Abstract: Enterprise strategic decision support requires AI systems that are not only accurate, but also uncertainty-aware, risk-calibrated, explainable, and governance-compliant. This paper proposes TRUST-ESD, a risk-calibrated and governance-aware framework for enterprise decision support under uncertainty. TRUST-ESD evaluates feasible counterfactual strategies through predictive utility estimation, conformal uncertainty calibration, CVaR-based downside-risk scoring, risk-memory retrieval, policy-as-code governance, explainability, and human oversight. Unlike prediction-only methods that select actions by maximum expected utility, TRUST-ESD recommends strategies that balance value, reliability, risk exposure, and compliance. Experimental results show that TRUST-ESD improves risk-adjusted utility by 7.95%, reduces risk exposure by 23.22%, reduces CVaR by 23.78%, lowers calibration error by 13.89%, improves explanation fidelity by 10.90%, and increases governance compliance by 9.76% compared with strong uncertainty-aware baselines, while maintaining competitive predictive accuracy. Ablation and case-study analyses further confirm that uncertainty calibration, downside-risk scoring, risk memory, explainability, and governance validation jointly improve trustworthy enterprise decision-making.

</details>


### [49] [CUSUM-Shaped Inference-Time Monitoring and Targeted Re-Decoding for Quantized Small Language Model Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20129)
*El Hassane Ettifouri,Ayoub Belfatmi,Mahaman Sanoussi Yahaya Alassan,Walid Dahhane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MGT-B（监控引导的测试时回溯）方法，通过监测自回归推理模型的不确定性和退化特征，在检测到异常轨迹时回溯到合适的回滚点并重新解码。在MATH-500数据集上的审计集显示准确率提升+2.50个百分点（McNemar p=0.2632），探索性分析显示+4.50个百分点（McNemar p=0.000753），但结果非确认性，不支持通用或理论保证的推理改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化小规模自回归推理模型在推理过程中容易陷入长序列、重复或低效的轨迹，但当前的推理时计算分配通常不观察轨迹的实际发展情况。现有方法缺乏对推理轨迹的实时监控和自适应修正机制。

Method: 基于早期的token级e-CUSUM控制器，提出MGT-B方法：1) 将重叠窗口中的采样前不确定性和退化特征映射到位置条件经验尾概率；2) 使用CUSUM形状的重置累积混合投注因子；3) 触发警报时估计回滚点，恢复token和KV缓存状态，执行约束重新解码。为了审计效果，构建了240对的chronology审计集和467对的历史覆盖集。

Result: 审计集上准确率从82/240提升至88/240（+2.50个百分点，13次修正、7次回归，McNemar p=0.2632，配对bootstrap 95%区间[-1.25, +6.25]）。历史覆盖集上从146/467提升至167/467（+4.50个百分点，McNemar p=0.000753），其中316条无警报输出与原始输出完全相同，151条警报轨迹中有29次修正和8次回归。

Conclusion: 结果支持在MATH-500设定下的选择性监控与修复机制，而非通用或理论上可保证的推理改进。两种分析均非确认性，经验因子尚未被确立为有效的e过程或e检测器。

Abstract: Quantized small autoregressive reasoning models can enter long, repetitive, or unproductive trajectories, yet inference-time compute is usually allocated without observing how a trajectory develops. Building on an earlier token-level e-CUSUM controller, we develop MGT-B (Monitoring-Guided Test-time Backtracking), a revised external controller that maps overlapping windows of pre-sampling uncertainty and degeneration features to position-conditional empirical tail probabilities, accumulates mixture betting factors with a CUSUM-shaped reset, and responds to an alarm by estimating a rollback point, restoring token and key-value-cache state, and performing constrained re-decoding. To audit whether the effect persists on problem identities first observed after the manual choice of log threshold h = 10, we retrospectively exclude 260 IDs present in pre-threshold artifacts and retain the chronologically first post-threshold pair for each remaining ID, yielding a 240-pair chronology-audit set. On this set, accuracy changes from 82/240 to 88/240 (+2.50 percentage points; 13 corrections, 7 regressions; exact McNemar p = 0.2632; paired bootstrap 95% interval [-1.25, +6.25]). A broader 467-pair historical-coverage set of seed-matched pairs changes accuracy from 146/467 to 167/467 (+4.50 points; McNemar p = 0.000753), but includes 200 seed-1 IDs available before or during threshold selection and is reported only as an exploratory estimate. All 316 no-alarm outputs in the 467-pair set are identical to vanilla, while the 151 alarmed trajectories contain 29 corrections and 8 regressions. Neither analysis is confirmatory, and the empirical factors are not established as a valid e-process or e-detector. The results support a selective monitoring-and-repair mechanism for the studied MATH-500 setting, rather than a general or theoretically certified reasoning improvement.

</details>


### [50] [PoTRE: Test-Time Reasoning inspired by Cognitive Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20268)
*Anmol Kankariya,Sercan Ö. Arık*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PoTRE是一个将推理过程解耦为四个异构代理（对抗性精炼、分层策略规划、谱系搜索、直接链式）的框架，通过任务自适应聚合层动态整合多视角结果，在ARC-AGI-2、HLE和PRBench Finance三个前沿基准上取得SOTA性能，其中HLE上达到49.92%的准确率，且相比同等规模的同质化基线使用更少或相当的推理token实现了更优的推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在需要长程规划与迭代纠错的复杂推理任务上表现脆弱，标准单流提示方法在面对新颖抽象或严格领域约束时不够鲁棒。

Method: 提出PoTRE框架，将推理解耦为四个异构代理（对抗性精炼、分层策略规划、谱系搜索、直接链式），并通过任务自适应聚合层（包括最终候选选择、语义合成或神经符号验证）动态整合各视角得到全局鲁棒解。

Result: 在ARC-AGI-2、HLE和PRBench Finance三个基准上取得SOTA性能，HLE上准确率达49.92%超越此前最佳官方成绩，且使用相似或更少推理token即优于大规模同质化基线。

Conclusion: 架构异构性能够以更少的推理开销实现更优的复杂推理性能，验证了多视角解耦与动态聚合策略的有效性。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) excel at many tasks, they frequently struggle with complex reasoning that requires long-horizon planning and iterative error correction. Furthermore, standard single-stream prompting proves brittle when models encounter novel abstractions or rigorous domain constraints. We introduce PoTRE (Poly-Topological Reasoning Ensembles), a heterogeneous framework that decouples inference into four agents: (1) Adversarial Refinement Agent, (2) Hierarchical strategic Planning Agent, (3) Spectrum Search Agent, and (4) Direct Chain Agent. A final Task-Adaptive Aggregation Layer dynamically reconciles these perspectives -- via final candidate selection, semantic synthesis, or neuro-symbolic verification -- to produce a robust global solution. We evaluate PoTRE on three frontier benchmarks: ARC-AGI-2, Humanity's Last Exam (HLE), and PRBench Finance. PoTRE achieves state-of-the-art accuracy of 49.92% on HLE, surpassing the previous best official score. We demonstrate that this architectural heterogeneity achieves improved reasoning performance using similar or fewer inference tokens compared to heavily scaled homogeneous baselines.

</details>


### [51] [Train the Model, Not the Reader: Decodability Supervision for Verifiable Activation Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20379)
*Hiskias Dingeto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了自然语言自编码器对隐藏层激活的解释评估存在根本缺陷：高重构分数并不保证解释中具体声明的真实性。作者发现标准方法会导致解释与模型形成共适应的私有编码（虚假措辞），约98%的具体声明与重构无关，分数仅反映大意而非事实。为此提出两套审计协议和RECAP方法——通过在目标模型旁训练线性头来保持指定内容可解码，使解释能真实陈述指定内容且消除私有编码。实验表明RECAP在Qwen-2.5-7B、Pythia-160M等模型上有效，使独立探针能可靠区分解释中的真伪声明（AUC 0.96），并能抵抗对抗性谎言攻击（AUC 0.95 vs 对照组的0.51）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自然语言自编码器通过重构分数评估解释的忠实性，但该方法对单个虚假声明不敏感——翻转某个声明若不改变重构结果则不会受到惩罚。作者旨在揭示这一结构性缺陷，并提出更可靠的审计方法和改进方案，以确保可解释性方法能真正反映模型内部状态，而非产生虚假的忠实性表象。

Method: 1) 提出两套审计协议：grounded-vs-true cross和evaluator swap，用于检测解释是否与模型形成共适应私有编码；2) 提出RECAP方法，在目标模型训练的同时训练线性辅助预测头，使指定内部内容保持可解码状态；3) 在Qwen-2.5-7B verbalizer、合成真实数据、Pythia-160M等场景下进行验证；4) 设计对抗性攻击实验（编辑解释以最大化重构分数同时撒谎）测试RECAP的鲁棒性。

Result: 1) 在Qwen-2.5-7B上，解释重构远高于随机水平，但仅~2%的具体声明是重构依赖的，分数跟踪的是大意而非具体事实；2) 在合成真实数据下，标准方法在5/5次运行中都发展出共适应私有编码；3) RECAP训练的沙盒模型上，新解释能真实陈述指定内容，私有编码消失，代价仅+0.001 nat；4) 在Pythia-160M上，指定内容变得可被探针可靠解码，但新解释仅部分传达（真实度0.44-0.46 vs 对照接近0）；5) 独立探针区分真伪声明AUC达0.96（无RECAP为0.82），对抗攻击下AUC保持0.95（对照降至0.51）。

Conclusion: 高重构分数不能认证解释中具体声明的真实性，这对可解释性研究有重要警示。RECAP方法通过使指定内部内容可被独立探针检查（而非依赖模型可能操纵的文本解释），为AI安全提供了更可靠的机制——独立探针能有效检测解释中的谎言，即使在面对针对性的对抗攻击时也能保持高检测性能。这意味着在设计可解释性系统时，需要将内部表示的独立可验证性作为关键设计目标。

Abstract: Natural-language autoencoders score explanations of hidden activations by reconstruction: an explanation is deemed faithful if the activation can be regenerated from it. The test is structurally insensitive to individual false claims: if flipping a claim does not change the reconstruction, the claim is never penalized. We show the test is passed in two ways, neither faithful. On a released Qwen-2.5-7B verbalizer, explanations reconstruct well above chance while ~2% of specific claims are reconstruction-dependent, so the score tracks gist, not specific facts. Under exact synthetic ground truth, the standard recipe develops co-adapted private codes (false wording the reconstruction depends on) in 5/5 runs, and fixes that leave the target model unchanged do not help. We contribute two audit protocols, the grounded-vs-true cross and the evaluator swap, and RECAP (Readable Encodings via Co-trained Auxiliary Predictors): linear heads trained alongside the target model to keep designated content decodable. On RECAP-trained sandbox models, fresh verbalizers state the designated content truly and the codes vanish, at a +0.001-nat cost. This replicates on a pretrained Pythia-160M: the content becomes reliably probe-decodable, though a fresh verbalizer conveys it only in part (truth 0.44-0.46 vs a near-zero control). For interpretability, high reconstruction does not certify individual claims. For AI safety, RECAP makes designated internal content independently checkable against probes rather than asserted by prose a model can game: an independent probe scores the verbalizer's true claims above its false ones (AUC 0.96, vs 0.82 without RECAP). Against an adversary that edits an explanation to maximize the reconstruction score while lying (suppressing ~87% of its lie penalty), the RECAP probe still flags the lies (AUC 0.95) while the control probe collapses to chance (0.51).

</details>


### [52] [SoftReason: A Fully Differentiable Neuro-Soft-Symbolic Deductive Reasoning Architecture over High-Dimensional Perceptual Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20402)
*Wael AbdAlmageed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SoftReason是一种神经软符号架构，通过可微的演绎推理将感知网络与知识图谱证据无缝连接，将推理状态表示为连续软解释张量，并在KVQA任务上实现端到端训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统神经符号方法中感知与演绎之间存在离散接口的梯度断裂问题，无法实现端到端可微训练。

Method: 提出可微的即时后果算子（immediate-consequence operator）的提升方法，使用谓词定义嵌入和潜在组合通道形成软体谓词混合，通过单调概率OR聚合所有可能证据并更新解释状态。

Result: 在知识感知视觉问答（KVQA）任务上实现了感知接地、知识图谱证据注入和可微演绎闭包的一体化端到端训练。

Conclusion: SoftReason弥合了感知与推理之间的梯度鸿沟，为结合高维输入与结构化知识的可微推理提供了可行的框架。

Abstract: In many reasoning problems, the premises are not observed as discrete symbols, but must be inferred from high-dimensional inputs. Further, the predicate vocabulary, argument structure, and trusted evidence are supplied by a Knowledge Graph (KG), or rule definitions. Classical neuro-symbolic pipelines have a discrete interface between perception and deduction. We present a neuro-soft-symbolic architecture for differentiable deductive reasoning over latent perceptual facts and knowledge-provided predicates. SoftReason removes the gradient gap by representing the deductive state as a local soft interpretation tensor over candidate constants and predicates. Perception proposes probabilistic base facts, KG triples enter as high-confidence soft evidence, and every query anchor, predicate choice, and closure update remains differentiable. Our core innovation is a learned differentiable lift of the immediate-consequence operator. It uses predicate-definition embeddings and latent composition channels to form soft body-predicate mixtures, aggregate over all possible witnesses, propose query-conditioned head facts, and update the interpretation through a monotone probabilistic OR. We instantiate the framework on Knowledge-aware Visual Question Answering (KVQA), and demonstrates how SoftReason supports end-to-end perceptual grounding, KG evidence injection, and differentiable deductive closure in one trainable architecture.

</details>

### [53] [Equilibrium Causal Games: Separation, Identification, and the Identifiability of Cyclic Latent States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19531)
*Faraz Dadgostari,Neda Nazemi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出均衡因果博弈（ECG）框架，系统研究了在未知传感、隐藏输入和循环因果结构下，从均衡数据中识别因果关系的可能性与局限性，给出了多种条件下可识别性与不可识别性的理论边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的电力网络、市场系统和互动种群等复杂系统往往处于反馈驱动的均衡状态，观测数据通过未知传感器采集，且存在隐藏输入和循环因果结构。传统因果发现方法难以处理这类场景，因此需要建立新的理论框架来阐明哪些因果结论可以从均衡数据中得出，哪些必须依赖针对性实验。

Method: 提出Equilibrium Causal Game (ECG)框架，将博弈论与循环因果模型、隐藏输入、传感器映射及干预规则相结合。利用后门准则/半途路径进行可识别性分析，通过旋转对称高斯块、线性非高斯(LiNG)模型、非线性传感等不同设定进行理论推导。

Result: ECG分离在给定条件下是可靠但不完全的；被动稳定线性模型中未知连接与满秩未知传感导致B完全不可识别(d≥2)；非高斯性可消除源旋转歧义；机制干预可分离开传感与相互作用；在特定条件下d-1个目标干预即可满足；非线性传感下各向同性高斯源块存在隐藏扭曲；在正性等条件下可识别最细独立源块表示至块置换与块内坐标变换。

Conclusion: 系统给出了均衡数据下因果推断的边界：部分结论可直接从观测数据获得，但许多关键因果关系（如传感器与相互作用的拆分、下游机制）必须依赖设计良好的目标干预实验才能识别。

Abstract: Power grids, markets, and interacting populations, settle into feedback driven equilibria observed through unknown sensors. Our Equilibrium Causal Game (ECG) joins a game to its cyclic causal model, hidden inputs, sensor map, and rules for interventions and equilibrium selection; interventions edit declared objects and recompute equilibrium. Under stated conditions, ECG-separation is sound but incomplete in our examples. Back-door/half-trek routes identify observed queries. Yet for an untouched rotationally symmetric Gaussian block, second moments determine only a source-frame rotation, across which distinct-variable effects generically change. Unknown sensing creates a separate ambiguity. In passive stable linear models without self-effects, unknown wiring and full-rank unknown sensing leave $B$ completely unidentified for $d\ge2$. Under LiNG, non-Gaussianity removes the source rotation; mechanism interventions separate sensing from interactions. With unknown support, invariant sensing, aligned responses, and well-posed single-target interventions identify $(H,B)$ up to declared equivalence. Of $d$ targets, $d-1$ suffice exactly when the sole untargeted node directly parents all others; otherwise $d$ are needed. Acquisition probes are excluded; known wiring gives no universal count. With nonlinear sensing, isotropic Gaussian source blocks admit hidden twists within and across blocks in labelled environments preserving required radial laws. Conversely, under stated positivity, informative one-block changes, rank, and irreducibility conditions, the finest independent source-block representation is identified within the stated alternative class up to block permutation and blockwise coordinate changes, but not downstream mechanisms or the sensor/interaction split. Together, these results show which causal conclusions equilibrium data support and which require targeted experiments.

</details>


### [54] [Online Optimization of Difference-of-Convex Compositions with Smooth Mappings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19553)
*Jingwei Ji,Jong-Shi Pang,Renyuan Xu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对一类结构化的非凸非光滑在线优化问题（损失函数为差分凸函数与光滑映射的复合，可行域由同类约束定义），提出了时间平滑近端线性算法，并基于近端残差映射定义了局部遗憾度量。理论证明了该残差是一阶平稳性的合理度量，利用切线锥刻画了复合差分凸约束的可行域，在非凸问题下仍可通过凸优化预言机计算更新，给出了局部遗憾界和内部凸子问题总数界，并导出了近端残差到平稳性距离的误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的在线优化方法主要针对凸问题或简单非凸问题，对于结构化非凸非光滑问题（如差分凸函数与光滑映射的复合）缺乏有效的理论框架和算法。本文旨在填补这一空白，为这类广泛存在的结构化非凸问题提供可理论保证的在线优化方法。

Method: 1) 提出时间平滑近端线性算法（time-smoothed proximal linear algorithm）；2) 基于近端残差映射定义局部遗憾度量；3) 利用切线锥刻画复合差分凸约束的可行域，使每次更新可通过凸优化预言机求解；4) 建立局部遗憾界和内部凸子问题总数界；5) 导出近端残差到平稳性距离的误差界。

Result: 1) 证明了近端残差的固定点条件等价于一阶平稳性，表明该残差是合理的平稳性度量；2) 建立了局部遗憾界；3) 给出了总内部凸子问题数的界；4) 导出了近端残差到平稳性距离的定量误差界，为近似平稳性提供了定量验证。

Conclusion: 本文为结构化的非凸非光滑在线优化问题提供了首个具有理论保障的算法框架，其中切线锥刻画和凸优化预言机求解策略具有独立价值，可推广至更广泛的非凸优化场景。

Abstract: We study online optimization for a broad class of structured non-convex non-smooth problems where each loss is a composition of a difference-of-convex function with a smooth mapping, and the feasible region is defined by constraint functions of the same kind.
  We propose a time-smoothed proximal linear algorithm and a local-regret measure based on a proximal residual mapping. We show that this residual is a proper stationarity measure for the original problem: its fixed-point condition implies first-order stationarity.
  Our analysis relies on a tangent-cone characterization for a feasible region described by composite difference-of-convex constraints, which is of independent interest and allows each update to be computed via a convex optimization oracle, despite the non-convexity of the problem.
  We establish a local-regret bound and a bound on the total number of inner convex subproblems.
  We also derive an error bound connecting the proximal residual to the distance to stationarity, providing a quantitative certificate of approximate stationarity.

</details>

Abstract: In this paper, a data-driven method for the computation of stabilizing state-feedbacks is proposed that leads to a rectified eigenstructure of switched linear systems. This means that all switching subsystems have the same sets of eigenvectors and the rectification allows to compute a common quadratic Lyapunov function ensuring asymptotic stability of the closed-loop system. The method is illustrated for two examples including a model of an aerosonde.

Abstract: Motivated by stochastic model predictive control applications, we present a semi-algebraic approach to constraint tightening for chance-constrained systems with unbounded additive disturbances and saturated inputs. Abstract: We introduce an open-source library for machine-checked control theory in the interactive proof assistant Lean to lay foundations for the verification of cyber-physical systems. Abstract: Conditional optimization tailors decisions to contextual or event information, but its practical use is often limited by the difficulty of learning the relevant conditional distribution from finite samples of a target joint distribution. This challenge is especially acute when target joint data are scarce or unavailable, or when few observations fall in the conditioning region of interest. Related joint data may be available from multiple sources, such as different stores, markets, populations, or operating environments, but these sources may be biased relative to the target distribution and cannot be pooled naively. We develop a distributionally robust framework based on optimal transport (OT) for harnessing such heterogeneous data in conditional optimization. The framework constructs ambiguity sets over joint distributions using OT distances to empirical source distributions and optimizes worst-case conditional performance over plausible target laws. We propose three OT ambiguity sets that capture different ways of using heterogeneous sources: enforcing simultaneous source consistency, aggregating source discrepancies through weights, and centering the ambiguity set at an OT barycenter. We derive tractable reformulations, establish feasibility conditions, discuss parameter choices, and characterize the relationships among the formulations, revealing trade-offs between robustness, information aggregation, and computational complexity. We demonstrate the value of the framework through a conditional assortment problem using demand and product-feature data from multiple stores.

</details>

Abstract: We develop a non-parametric approach to refine forecast-error histories for safety stock estimation without relying on error magnitude or recency alone. Using forecast-error data from a fast-moving consumer goods environment, we show that one year of errors is insufficient to characterize service-level risk, while pooling several years is problematic because error distributions change over time. Non-parametric annual comparisons indicate a progressive reduction in bias and dispersion, consistent with learning in the forecasting process. We propose LOWDII (Leave-One-Out Wasserstein Distributional Influence Index), a diagnostic that evaluates the distributional influence of each historical forecast error on the empirical uncertainty distribution used for safety stock calibration. LOWDII identifies observations whose influence is disproportionate to their representativeness, separating transient distortions from persistent tail behaviour without imposing parametric assumptions. We evaluate the method in a discrete-event simulation using subsequent-year demand realizations as validation. The results show that LOWDII achieves the target service level while reducing average stock by 5.1% to 22.6% relative to benchmark methods. From a managerial perspective, LOWDII helps firms translate improvements in the forecasting process into safety stock decisions, avoiding the projection of obsolete historical errors into the future and capturing the economic benefit of lower uncertainty requirements earlier.

Abstract: We study continuous-time reinforcement learning for $N$-player noncooperative stochastic differential games. We then derive an explicit, checkable sufficient condition for the tightness of the order-$2$ block-sparse SOS relaxation associated with the connected-component decomposition of this graph. The key difficulty, which is absent from the edgeless case, is establishing tightness for a two-unit edge block. Isolated-vertex blocks reduce to one-unit problems over box input sets and are therefore handled at the same order. Motivation: 现有求解单调包含问题的算法（如FBF、FRB）存在以下局限：1）依赖全局Lipschitz常数估计，计算成本高且步长受限；2）FBF每次迭代需两次算子评估，计算效率低；3）现有FRB线搜索仅对局部Lipschitz算子保证收敛，对连续算子可能无法终止。因此需要一种对连续算子也能保证收敛且计算高效的算法。

Method: 提出一种新颖的FRB算法线搜索策略，该策略在算子仅为连续时也能保证良好定义和收敛性。进一步将算法扩展以处理额外的cocoercive和Lipschitz连续算子。

Result: 数值实验在鞍点问题和图像恢复任务上进行。结果表明：1）所提线搜索FRB在算子为Lipschitz连续时仍能加速数值收敛；2）与线搜索FBF相比具有竞争力，且在多种场景下具有显著的计算优势（每次迭代仅需一次算子评估）。

Conclusion: 提出的线搜索策略使FRB算法适用于更广泛的连续算子场景，克服了现有方法的收敛保证局限，同时保持了计算效率优势，为单调包含问题提供了更实用的求解方案。

Abstract: In this article, we aim to solve a monotone inclusion problem involving the sum of a maximally monotone operator and a continuous operator. While several algorithms exist to solve this problem when the continuous operator is cocoercive or Lipschitz continuous, they typically require the estimation of the global Lipschitz constant, which can be computationally expensive and often imposes overly restrictive step-sizes. To avoid these limitations and to handle merely continuous operators, linesearch subroutines are employed. A popular method in this context is the forward-backward-forward (FBF) algorithm (also known as Tseng's splitting), which utilizes a linesearch to guarantee convergence. However, a drawback of FBF is that the continuous operator must be evaluated twice per iteration. On the other hand, the forward-reflected-backward (FRB) algorithm proposed by Malitsky and Tam (2020) requires only a single evaluation of the operator per iteration. Although a linesearch version of FRB exists, its convergence is guaranteed only for locally Lipschitz operators; in fact, we present an example demonstrating that this existing linesearch can fail to terminate when the operator is merely continuous. In this work, we propose a novel linesearch strategy for FRB that is well defined and guarantees convergence even when the operator is merely continuous. We also extend the proposed algorithm to handle additional cocoercive and Lipschitz continuous operators. Finally, we provide numerical experiments on saddle-point problems and image restoration. The numerical results show that FRB with the proposed linesearch can accelerate the numerical convergence even when the operator is Lipschitz continuous. In addition, these results show that the proposed method is competitive with linesearch FBF, offering considerable computational advantages in various scenarios.

</details>

*Shengda Zeng,Jia Chen,Vo Thanh Phat*

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类耦合非光滑动力系统，其中半线性演化方程与由法锥到时变凸集控制的隐式代数律相耦合。通过变换变量将冻结代数律重述为凸集上的变分不等式，在强伪单调、Lipschitz连续假设下建立了状态-控制映射的灵敏度估计。利用C₀-半群估计与Bielecki不动点论证，证明了有限时间区间上温和解对的全局存在唯一性与Hadamard适定性，并推导了耗散半群 regime 下的增量指数稳定性准则以及参数-解映射的连续性。最后通过简化的接触力学实例说明了变分不等式公式化及显式投影残差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有耦合非光滑动力系统研究大多要求代数律具有显式反馈形式，而本文所处理的问题中代数变量需通过状态依赖的法锥关系隐式恢复，这带来了本质困难。本文旨在为这类更一般的耦合系统建立严格的分析框架，包括解的存在唯一性、适定性及稳定性理论。

Method: 采用变换变量将冻结代数律转化为凸集上的变分不等式；在强伪单调和Lipschitz连续假设下分析冻结问题的代数响应唯一性与灵敏度估计；利用C₀-半群估计和Bielecki不动点技术证明耦合系统的全局存在唯一性；通过连续依赖性估计建立Hadamard适定性；在耗散半群框架下推导增量指数稳定性；借助投影残差公式化接触力学实例。

Result: 证明了冻结代数问题在强伪单调、Lipschitz连续假设下存在唯一代数响应并具有状态-控制映射的灵敏度估计（无需投影-收缩论证）；给出了该假设的两类可验证充分条件（加权强单调构造和标准Lipschitz小性条件）；建立了耦合系统在有限时间区间上温和解对的全局存在唯一性与Hadamard适定性；获得了耗散半群 regime 下的增量指数稳定性准则；证明了参数-解映射关于初值和外部参数的连续性；提供了接触力学中的变分不等式公式化与投影残差表达式。

Conclusion: 本文为状态依赖法锥约束的耦合非光滑动力系统提供了完整的分析理论基础，包括解的存在唯一性、适定性、稳定性及参数依赖性。所提框架允许强伪单调非单调的冻结算子，拓宽了适用范围。接触力学实例展示的方法为后续离散化后的投影法和Newton型内层求解器提供了可行起点。

Abstract: This paper studies a coupled nonsmooth dynamical system in which a semilinear evolution equation is coupled with an implicit algebraic law governed by the normal cone to a time-dependent convex set. The main difficulty is that the algebraic variable is not given by an explicit feedback, but must be recovered from a state-dependent normal cone relation. Using a transformed variable, we recast each frozen algebraic law as a variational inequality over a convex set. Under a strongly pseudomonotone, Lipschitz continuous hypothesis, this frozen problem has a unique algebraic response and admits a sensitivity estimate for the state-to-control map without a projection-contraction argument. We also give verifiable sufficient conditions for this hypothesis, including a weighted strongly monotone construction and a standard Lipschitz-smallness condition, and point out that the framework allows strongly pseudomonotone nonmonotone frozen operators. The coupled system is then reduced to a semilinear evolution equation with a single state variable. By combining $C_0$-semigroup estimates with a Bielecki fixed-point argument, we prove global existence and uniqueness of mild solution pairs on finite time intervals and establish Hadamard well-posedness through explicit continuous-dependence estimates. We further derive an incremental exponential stability criterion in the dissipative semigroup regime and prove continuity of the parameter-to-solution map with respect to the initial datum and an external parameter. A reduced contact-mechanics example illustrates the variational inequality formulation and an explicit projection residual that can be used as a starting point for projection- and Newton-type inner solvers after discretization.

</details>

*Zhanyuan Cai,Emre Sahinoglu,Shahin Shahrampour*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了黎曼流形上强测地凸损失的分布式在线优化，首次在分布式场景下实现了$O(\log T)$的静态遗憾界，匹配欧氏空间强凸优化的最优速率，并扩展到了两点带隙反馈设置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式黎曼优化仅处理一般测地凸损失，强测地凸情形未被探索；集中式强测地凸优化可将遗憾从$O(\sqrt{T})$降至$O(\log T)$，但分布式场景中所需的衰减步长与现有基于固定步长的网络误差分析不兼容。

Method: 首先提出适用于时变调度的一般性网络误差分析框架；基于此分析，证明分布式在线黎曼梯度下降法在强测地凸损失下达到$O(\log T)$静态遗憾界；进一步利用强次凸性论证，证明两点带隙反馈设置下平滑损失函数同样达到$O(\log T)$遗憾界。

Result: 首次在分布式黎曼优化中为强测地凸损失建立了$O(\log T)$的静态遗憾界，匹配欧氏空间强凸优化的极小化最优速率，并推广至两点带隙反馈场景。

Conclusion: 本文填补了分布式黎曼优化在强测地凸损失上的理论空白，证明了在具有有界截面曲率（包括正曲率）的黎曼流形上，分布式在线梯度下降法在完全反馈和两点带隙反馈下均能达到最优的$O(\log T)$遗憾界。

Abstract: We study decentralized online optimization for strongly geodesically convex (strongly g-convex) losses on Riemannian manifolds with bounded sectional curvature, including positively curved manifolds. In centralized Riemannian optimization, strong g-convexity tightens the optimal regret from $O(\sqrt{T})$ to $O(\log T)$, where $T$ is the time horizon; in the decentralized Riemannian setting, however, existing methods address only g-convex losses, leaving the strongly g-convex regime unexplored. One challenge is that the required decaying step size in the centralized regime is incompatible with existing network-error analyses, which typically assume a fixed step size. First, we provide a general network-error analysis for time-varying schedules. Next, we build on this analysis to establish the first $O(\log T)$ static regret bound for decentralized online Riemannian gradient descent, matching the minimax-optimal rate for strongly-convex Euclidean online optimization. Finally, we prove the same $O(\log T)$ regret bound for the two-point bandit feedback setting using novel strong subconvexity arguments for the smoothed versions of the loss functions.

</details>

Abstract: We consider the numerical solution of shape optimization problems for thermoelasticity with temperature-dependent material parameters. We show the existence of the shape derivative and derive an expression for generic functionals of domain integral type. Numerical results are presented for two settings: minimization of the compliance under a volume constraint, and minimization of the volume under a constraint on the $L^2$-norm of the von Mises stress. We use the finite element method for solving the underlying boundary value problems and the level set method for the representation of the actual domain.

</details>

Abstract: We prove strong laws of large numbers for locally Lipschitz functions in the Lipschitz pseudometric. Our results hold under either a topological or a model-theoretic condition, with the latter encompassing functions jointly definable in o-minimal structures but extending substantially beyond this class. Applications include uniform convergence of limiting and Clarke subdifferentials and finite-sample identification of solutions. Consequently, we identify broad classes of functions for which the failure phenomena revealed by our previous negative results [Tian and Royset, arXiv:2511.16568, 2025] do not occur.

</details>

### [71] [A Bayesian Framework for Built-in Input Dimension Reduction for Gaussian Process Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19498)

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种贝叶斯框架，将高斯过程建模与降维无缝集成，通过在Stiefel流形上设置先验并利用哈密顿蒙特卡洛进行后验推断，同时扩展至深度高斯过程，在提高预测性能和不确定性量化方面优于传统两阶段方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯过程在高维输入下受维度灾难影响严重，现有降维方法通常采用两阶段策略（先降维再GP拟合），无法实现联合推断，导致信息丢失和不确定性传播不充分。

Method: 提出层次贝叶斯模型，在Stiefel流形上对投影矩阵施加正交性先验，通过带测地流哈密顿蒙特卡洛进行后验推断；进一步扩展至深度高斯过程，实现内置降维。

Result: 所提贝叶斯方法虽然计算成本更高，但在预测性能和不确定性量化方面优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种原则性且稳健的替代方案，适用于高维输入下的GP建模场景。

Abstract: Gaussian process (GP) modeling is widely used in computational science and engineering. However, fitting a GP to high-dimensional inputs remains challenging due to the curse of dimensionality. While various methods have been proposed to reduce input dimensionality, they typically follow a two-stage approach, performing dimension reduction and GP fitting separately. We introduce a Bayesian framework that seamlessly integrates dimensionality reduction with GP modeling and inference. Our approach, built on a hierarchical Bayesian model with priors on the Stiefel manifold, enforces orthonormality on the projection matrix and enables posterior inference via Hamiltonian Monte Carlo with geodesic flow. Additionally, we extend this framework by incorporating Deep Gaussian Processes (DGP) with built-in dimension reduction, providing a more flexible and powerful tool for complex datasets. Through extensive numerical studies, we demonstrate that while the proposed Bayesian method incurs higher computational costs, it improves predictive performance and uncertainty quantification, providing a principled and robust alternative to existing methods.

</details>


### [72] [Boltzmann-Expected Molecular Design with Decoupled Annealing Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19519)
*Selma Moqvist,Richard Beckmann,Ross Irwin,Rocío Mercado,Simon Olsson*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出DECAF（解耦退火流），通过将分子图与3D构象的联合分布分解为两个条件流模型，实现了基于玻尔兹曼期望的3D分子设计。该方法以系综统计而非单构象性质为优化目标，支持多目标优化和高阶矩设计（如方差和偏度），在GEOM-Drugs数据集上优于传统单构象方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有性质导向的生成模型将每个性质绑定到单一结构，忽略了分子3D构象的玻尔兹曼分布，导致对柔性大分子的设计效果不佳。因此需要一种能够基于系综期望进行分子设计的方法。

Method: 将联合分布分解为图条件流p(x|G)（玻尔兹曼模拟器）和坐标条件流p(G|x)（从3D信息生成新图），通过交替两个流并采用模拟退火接受规则，以系综分布上的统计量（而非单构象性质）作为评分函数进行优化。

Result: 在GEOM-Drugs数据集上，系综感知优化使分子的回转半径和溶剂可及表面积一致地向目标偏移，而单构象优化在柔性药物分子上表现退化。DECAF支持多目标权衡和唯一的高阶矩设计（优化系综性质的方差和偏度），并通过全原子MD模拟验证了构象分布。

Conclusion: DECAF通过玻尔兹曼期望设计范式，实现了更物理真实的3D分子生成，尤其适用于柔性大分子，且无需重训练即可切换优化目标，拓展了分子设计的能力边界。

Abstract: Most 3D properties relevant to molecular design, including free energies and shape descriptors, are $\textit{expectations}$ over the Boltzmann distribution over 3D configurations of a molecular graph. However, existing property-guided generative models tie each property to a single structure, ignoring the underlying ensemble. We recast 3D molecular design as $\textbf{Boltzmann-expected design}$ and realise it with $\textbf{DECAF}$ (Decoupled Annealing Flows), which factorise the joint distribution over graphs and coordinates into two conditional flow models: a graph-conditioned flow $p(x\mid\mathcal{G})$, acting as a $\textit{Boltzmann emulator}$, and a coordinate-conditioned flow $p(\mathcal{G}\mid x)$, proposing new graphs from 3D information. By alternating the two flows, DECAF optimises molecular graphs with a simulated-annealing acceptance rule whose scoring function is evaluated on ensembles drawn from $p(x\mid\mathcal{G})$, making ensemble statistics, not single-conformer properties, the design target. The resulting loop requires no retraining to change objectives. On GEOM-Drugs, we show that ensemble-aware optimisation produces graphs whose mean radius of gyration and solvent-accessible surface area consistently shift toward targets, while single-conformer optimisation degrades on larger drug-like molecules where Boltzmann distributions are broadest. DECAF extends to multi-objective trade-offs and, uniquely among 3D generative models, to $\textbf{higher-moment design}$: jointly optimising an ensemble property's variance and skewness to produce flexible molecules biased to a prescribed conformational regime: we verify the conformational distributions of these higher-moment designs with all-atom MD simulations.

</details>

*Yiwei Zhou,Ziheng Chen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: RELTA-SGLD通过相对增长驯服方案稳定超线性随机梯度更新，在非凸优化中实现多项式矩稳定性和一阶平稳精度，并在Fashion-MNIST上优于未驯服SGLD和TUSLA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SGLD方法在处理超线性增长随机梯度时存在不稳定性问题，而现有的驯服方案过度抑制了原始学习漂移，导致收敛性能下降。

Method: 提出RELTA-SGLD驯服方案，通过阈值控制驯服启动时机，基于单步Lyapunov稳定性条件推导相对增长原则确定驯服强度，使用更轻量的λ尺度分母保留非零远尾回报。

Conclusion: RELTA-SGLD在稳定性和学习动态保留之间取得更好平衡，与调优后的AdamW竞争力相当，为超线性随机梯度的驯服提供了理论更优的解决方案。

Abstract: We introduce RELTA-SGLD, a taming scheme that stabilizes superlinear stochastic-gradient updates while reducing unnecessary suppression of the original learning drift. A threshold determines where the taming turns on, while a relative-growth principle derived from the one-step Lyapunov stability condition determines the required taming strength. Together, they produce a lighter $λ$-scale denominator and preserve a nonvanishing far-tail return. As a consequence, we prove polynomial moment stability and first-order stationary accuracy in both $W_1$ and $W_2$ for nonconvex SGLD with superlinearly growing stochastic-gradient oracles, improving the corresponding half-order and quarter-order bounds for comparable stochastic-gradient tamed schemes. On Fashion-MNIST under active stabilization pressure, RELTA improves the mean learning metrics over both untamed SGLD and TUSLA and remains competitive with a tuned AdamW reference. In an ordinary-training regime, its lighter localized denominator reduces unnecessary perturbation of the original update and maintains nearly untamed learning dynamics.

</details>


### [74] [Optimal Recalibration of an Online Predictor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19689)
*Lunjia Hu,Kevin Tian,Chutong Yang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究在线预测器的再校准问题，提出了一种基于非平衡Blackwell可逼近性约简的在线算法，实现了对Lipschitz proper loss的(ε, ε²)-再校准，并证明该权衡在平方损失下最优。作为主要应用，该算法与在线精炼方法结合，实现了同时的ε-校准和ε²-calibeating，改进了先前工作的ε依赖关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 给定任意"提示"预测序列，学习器需要输出新的校准预测，同时相对于原始预测在适当损失下保持较小的超额误差。现有方法在同时实现校准与calibeating方面存在依赖关系较差的问题。

Method: 提出了一种非平衡扩展的同步Blackwell可逼近性约减框架，基于此设计了在线再校准算法，在T ≈ ε⁻³轮内实现对Lipschitz proper loss的(ε, ε²)-再校准。此外，将该算法与在线精炼方法结合，实现同时校准与calibeating。

Conclusion: 本文在在线再校准问题上取得了最优的误差权衡，并展示了其在同时实现校准与calibeating方面的应用价值，回答了[CHJL26]中关于同时实现近最优calibeating和校准率的问题。

Abstract: We study the problem of recalibrating an online predictor [KE17, OKS24]: given an arbitrary "hint" sequence of forecasts, the learner must output new predictions that are calibrated while incurring small excess error relative to the original forecasts, under a proper loss. We give an online algorithm that achieves $(\varepsilon, \varepsilon^2)$-recalibration for Lipschitz proper losses in
  $T \approx \varepsilon^{-3}$ rounds, using an imbalanced extension of the recent simultaneous Blackwell approachability reduction framework of [HTY26]. We show that this tradeoff is optimal by proving a matching lower bound for recalibrating against the squared loss. We also prove a companion $\mathcal{K}_2$-recalibration theorem that obtains the same tradeoffs up to a logarithmic factor.
  As our main application, we show how our recalibration algorithms can be combined with the online refinement method of [FH23] to obtain simultaneous $\varepsilon$-calibration and $\varepsilon^2$-calibeating for smooth proper losses at the same asymptotic rate, improving upon prior works that achieved these properties separately or with a worse $\varepsilon$ dependence. In particular, the $\mathcal{K}_2$ variant answers a question of [CHJL26] on simultaneously achieving near-optimal calibeating and calibration rates. We also derive extensions to settings with multiple hint sequences. Finally, we empirically evaluate our algorithms on a classification dataset undergoing distribution shift.

</details>


### [75] [Data-Poisoning Audits for Causal Effect Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19692)
*Kwangho Kim*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文针对观察性因果分析中多源数据合并易受追加式数据投毒攻击的问题，提出了一种审计框架。该框架通过贪婪扫描算法计算给定追加预算下的最坏情况效应移动，并推导了包含直接贡献和通过倾向性/结果模型间接影响的总影响分数，还给出了完全重拟合估计的保守有限预算界。模拟和多站点/公开数据分析验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观察性因果分析越来越多地合并来自不同站点、供应商和收集系统的记录，这导致了易受"仅追加攻击"的漏洞——对手可以通过策略性地选择看似合理的记录来改变报告的处理效应。目前缺乏系统性的审计方法来量化这种数据投毒风险。

Method: 1) 提出数据投毒审计框架，适用于增强逆概率加权估计(AIPW)；2) 在预处理和干扰拟合固定的情况下，提出贪婪扫描算法，计算每个追加预算下的精确最坏情况效应移动；3) 推导总影响分数，结合每条记录的直接贡献及其通过倾向性模型和结果模型的间接影响；4) 获得完全重拟合估计的保守有限预算界。

Result: 1) 模拟验证了精确最坏情况效应移动计算的正确性；2) 总影响分数改进了局部重拟合预测；3) 多站点和公开数据分析表明，在较小的追加预算下就存在显著敏感性；4) 框架能够将对抗性数据组合风险转化为移动曲线和关键预算。

Conclusion: 该审计框架将对抗性数据组合风险量化为效应移动曲线和临界预算，能够支持更可靠的因果报告和源级安全保障的设计。

Abstract: Observational causal analyses increasingly pool records across sites, vendors, and collection systems, creating vulnerability to append-only attacks in which plausible records are strategically selected to alter a reported treatment effect. We develop a data-poisoning audit for augmented inverse-probability-weighted estimation. The analyst specifies a finite catalog of feasible records, an append budget, and nested source capacities, and the adversary selects a feasible subset to maximize movement in a prespecified direction. With preprocessing and nuisance fits held fixed, we propose a greedy scan that computes the exact finite-sample worst-case movement at every append budget. To account for nuisance refitting, we go on to derive a total-influence score combining each record's direct contribution with its effect through the propensity and outcome models. We further obtain a conservative finite-budget bound for the fully refitted estimate. Extensive simulations validate the exact result and show that total influence improves local refit prediction, while multisite and public-data analyses demonstrate material sensitivity at small append budgets. By translating adversarial data-composition risk into movement curves and critical budgets, the framework supports more reliable causal reporting and the design of source-level safeguards.

</details>


### [76] [Non--negative matrix factorization using the \textit{R} package \textsf{nnmf}](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20084)
*Volkan Sevinç,Nikolas Kontemeniotis,Theodoros Perdikis,Michail Tsagris*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个新的R语言NMF包，并在真实数据条件下与两个主流R包进行了系统性性能比较，评估指标包括计算效率、收敛行为、重建精度、内存利用率和分解稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有多个R包实现NMF，但在真实数据条件下的全面评估仍然有限，研究者缺乏客观的包选择指导。

Method: 提出一个新的R包，并与两个广泛使用的R包在真实数据集上进行系统比较，采用一致的实验框架评估计算效率、收敛行为、重建精度、内存利用率和分解稳定性。

Result: 在真实数据条件下对各包的计算效率、收敛行为、重建精度、内存利用率和分解稳定性进行了定量比较。

Conclusion: 新包在真实数据条件下表现出竞争力，为研究者提供了更客观的包选择依据。

Abstract: Non--negative matrix factorization (NMF) has become an established dimensionality reduction technique for extracting latent structures from non--negative data and has found widespread applications in fields such as bioinformatics, text mining, image analysis, and recommender systems. As the popularity of NMF has increased, numerous \textit{R} packages implementing different optimization strategies and computational frameworks have been developed. Despite their widespread availability, comprehensive evaluations of these implementations under real--world data conditions remain limited. Consequently, researchers often lack objective guidance when selecting an appropriate package for practical applications. This study introduces a new \textit{R} package for NMF and offers asystematic performance comparison with two widely available \textit{R} packages for NMF analysis. Rather than relying on simulated datasets, the evaluation is conducted using real--world data to better reflect the complexity, heterogeneity, and noise characteristics encountered in practical analytical settings. The packages are assessed using a consistent experimental framework, with emphasis on computational efficiency, convergence behavior, reconstruction accuracy, memory utilization, and the stability of the resulting matrix factorization.

</details>


### [77] [Directional Kernel Mean Difference: A Fast Signed Statistic for Univariate Distribution Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20119)
*Shijie Zhong,Jiangfeng Fu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出方向核均值差（DKMD），一种保留分布变化方向信息的有符号单变量分布比较统计量。与平方最大均值差异（MMD）不同，DKMD通过对固定奇权重函数积分核均值嵌入的差异来构建，具有反对称性、对对称分布差异的免疫性以及随机占优下的方向单调性。作者推导了数据驱动的Riemann估计器，确保渐进一致性，并开发了O(N log N)的前缀-后缀扫描算法，将计算复杂度从二次降至准线性。实验表明DKMD能正确隔离方向性变化与对称扰动，对重尾异常值具有鲁棒性，且可秒级处理百万样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布比较方法如平方MMD通过平方RKHS距离丢弃了方向信息，无法区分分布变化的方向（例如是左移还是右移）。作者希望设计一种既保留方向信息、又具备良好理论性质（反对称性、方向单调性）且计算高效的统计量。

Method: DKMD通过对核均值嵌入的差异在固定奇权重函数下积分来定义。提出数据驱动的Riemann估计器保证渐进一致性。开发了O(N log N)时间、O(N)空间的前缀-后缀扫描算法，利用实数轴的全序关系避免二次计算复杂度。

Result: 在合成基准测试中，DKMD能正确区分方向性偏移与对称扰动；对重尾异常值比均值差异更鲁棒（异常值不会翻转DKMD的符号）；可扩展至百万级样本，计算时间在秒级。

Conclusion: DKMD是一种有符号、方向感知、计算高效的分布比较工具，弥补了MMD等无符号统计量在方向信息上的缺失，特别适用于需要判断分布变化方向的场景。

Abstract: We introduce the Directional Kernel Mean Difference (DKMD), a signed statistic for univariate distribution comparison that preserves the direction of distributional shifts. Unlike the squared Maximum Mean Discrepancy (MMD), which discards directional information by squaring the RKHS distance, DKMD integrates the difference of kernel mean embeddings against a fixed odd weighting function. This construction yields three structural properties: antisymmetry, immunity to symmetric distributional differences, and directional monotonicity under stochastic dominance. We derive a data-driven Riemann estimator that ensures asymptotic consistency with the continuous formulation, strictly preserving the theoretical guarantees of the signed statistic in empirical evaluations. To overcome the quadratic computational cost of kernel methods, we develop an $O(N \log N)$ prefix--suffix scanning algorithm that exploits the total order of the real line while requiring only $O(N)$ memory. Experiments on synthetic benchmarks demonstrate that DKMD correctly isolates directional shifts from symmetric perturbations, remains robust to heavy-tailed outliers that can flip the sign of the mean difference, and scales to millions of samples in seconds.

</details>


### [78] [Statistical Inference for Rank Allocation in Low-Rank Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20205)
*Yihang Gao,Vincent Y. F. Tan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出StatLoRA，一种基于统计假设检验的LoRA秩分配方法，通过为每个LoRA组件构造检验统计量并使用p值决定保留或剪枝，在固定参数预算下实现自适应秩分配。实验在多个模型和任务上验证了其有效性，并提供了渐近正态性的理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自适应秩分配方法主要依赖基于梯度的敏感性或不确定性指标构造重要性分数，缺乏明确的统计解释。本文旨在将LoRA秩分配问题形式化为统计假设检验问题，提供具有严谨统计理论基础的自适应秩分配方法。

Method: 将LoRA秩分配建模为统计假设检验问题，为每个LoRA组件关联一个检验统计量，通过估计p值决定保留或剪枝。推导了包括AdamW在内的深度学习优化器轨迹的渐近正态性，并据此导出组件检验统计量的渐近分布。

Result: 在DeBERTaV3-base、BART-Large和Qwen2.5-7B上，覆盖自然语言理解、生成和问答任务，StatLoRA在匹配秩预算下性能优于或持平于vanilla LoRA、AdaLoRA和IGU-LoRA。敏感性分析和经验诊断支持假设检验分配规则的稳定性及渐近理论。

Conclusion: StatLoRA通过统计推断框架为LoRA自适应秩分配提供了理论严谨且实践有效的方案，确立了基于假设检验的分配规则的稳定性，并验证了深度学习优化器渐近理论的实际适用性。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) has become a widely used parameter-efficient fine-tuning method for large language models. Since different modules and layers may contribute unequally to downstream adaptation, allocating rank resources under a fixed parameter budget is an important problem for balancing efficiency, expressiveness, and generalization. Existing adaptive rank methods address this problem mainly through carefully designed importance scores constructed from gradient-derived sensitivity and uncertainty measures, without an explicit statistical interpretation. In this paper, we formulate LoRA rank allocation as a statistical hypothesis testing problem and propose StatLoRA, a statistical inference-based rank allocation method. StatLoRA associates each LoRA component with a test statistic and uses estimated p-values to determine which components should be retained or pruned under a prescribed rank budget. The proposed testing procedure is supported by our central limit theory for stochastic optimizer trajectories. In particular, we establish asymptotic normality for a broad class of commonly used optimizers in deep learning, including AdamW, and derive the corresponding asymptotic distributions for the proposed component scores used in hypothesis testing. We evaluate StatLoRA on LoRA fine-tuning of DeBERTaV3-base, BART-Large, and Qwen2.5-7B across natural language understanding, natural language generation, and question answering tasks. Experiments show that StatLoRA achieves comparable or better performance than vanilla LoRA, AdaLoRA, and IGU-LoRA under matched rank budgets. Sensitivity analyses and empirical diagnostics further support the stability of the proposed hypothesis-testing-based allocation rule and provide empirical evidence for the asymptotic theory of component scores.

</details>


### [79] [Adaptive Bayesian Online Learning via Expert Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20239)
*Jungbin Jun,Ilsang Ohn*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出将贝叶斯更新规则视为专家，通过顺序预测损失聚合贝叶斯专家，实现在线学习中的自适应推断。该方法在在线共形推断和高斯过程回归中应用，能够与事后最优专家竞争，并避免事先选择学习率、先验分布等推断选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯在线学习的性能严重依赖于学习率、先验分布和变分族等推断选择，这些通常需要在看到数据流之前固定，导致性能对初始设定敏感，缺乏自适应能力。

Method: 将不同的贝叶斯更新规则视为专家，根据顺序预测损失对贝叶斯专家进行聚合，聚合代价由每个专家每轮的表现评估方式决定。

Result: 在共形推断中得到平滑贝叶斯版本的自适应共形推断，具有长期随机化覆盖；在高斯过程回归中得到累积预测KL散度的oracle不等式，并实现对未知Hölder光滑性的自适应（对数因子内）。实验表明聚合能跟踪强专家，无需oracle专家选择。

Conclusion: 通过专家聚合框架实现了贝叶斯在线学习的自适应推断选择，无需先验固定超参数，理论上保证与最优专家竞争，实际效果良好。

Abstract: Bayesian online learning promises uncertainty-aware prediction on data streams, but its performance hinges on inferential choices, including learning rates, prior distributions and variational families, which are usually fixed before seeing the stream. We address this by treating Bayesian update rules as experts and aggregating the Bayesian experts according to sequential predictive losses. We prove that the resulting aggregate competes with the best expert in hindsight at an aggregation cost determined by how each expert's per-round performance is evaluated. We instantiate the framework in online conformal inference and Gaussian process regression. The conformal inference application yields a smoothed Bayesian counterpart of adaptive conformal inference with long-run randomized coverage, while the Gaussian process application gives an oracle inequality in cumulative predictive Kullback-Leibler risk and adaptation to unknown Hölder smoothness up to logarithmic factors. Experiments show that the aggregate tracks strong experts without oracle expert selection.

</details>


### [80] [Adaptive deep nonparametric regression from dependent data under covariate shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20309)
*William Kengne,Ehud Mossa Ockegna*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文针对协变量偏移和相依观测数据，提出了稀疏惩罚深度神经网络（SPDNN）估计器用于非参数分位数回归和Huber回归，并建立了非渐近误差界，证明其能自适应达到极小化最优收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际应用中源域和目标域数据分布不同（协变量偏移），标准度量不再适用，且观测数据可能具有相依性，现有方法缺乏对这类复杂场景的理论保证。

Method: 提出稀疏惩罚深度神经网络（SPDNN）估计器，利用密度比修正分布差异；对未知密度比采用两阶段预训练：先通过最小二乘SPDNN估计密度比，再用于重加权SPDNN估计回归函数；处理依赖数据时利用广义Bernstein型不等式。

Result: 建立了分位数回归和Huber回归下SPDNN估计器的非渐近误差界，证明其在Hölder光滑函数类中能自适应达到（至多对数因子）极小化最优收敛率，适用于i.i.d.数据及φ-混合、强混合、C-混合等时间序列模型。

Conclusion: 所提SPDNN方法在协变量偏移和相依观测下具有理论最优性，为深度神经网络在非标准数据场景下的非参数回归提供了理论保障。

Abstract: Covariate shift often occurs because, in many real applications, the source and the target observations may be generated from different distributions. In this case, the standard metric under the source distribution is not appropriate. This paper considers deep neural network estimators for nonparametric quantile and Huber regression under covariate shift and from dependent observations. We deal with a generalized Bernstein-type inequality that is satisfied by many classical models, including i.i.d. observations, $φ$-mixing, strong mixing, and $\mathcal{C}$-mixing processes. To perform the covariate shift phenomenon, we propose a sparse-penalized deep neural network (SPDNN) estimator that takes into account the discrepancy between the source and target distributions of the data. When the density ratio (between the source and target distributions of the covariate) is unknown, a two steps pre-training procedure is carried out: the first step is devoted to the construction of a least squares SPDNN estimator of the density ratio; which is used in the second step to perform a pre-training reweighted SPDNN estimator of the regression function. For both the quantile and the Huber regression, non-asymptotic error bounds of the proposed SPDNN estimators are established in the class of Hölder smooth functions. These estimators can adaptively attain (up to a logarithmic factor) the minimax optimal convergence rate from i.i.d. data as well as from several classical time series models.

</details>

### [81] [Native Multi-Dimensional Subquadratic Operators via Input Dependent Long Convolutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19378)
*David R. Wessels,Farhad Ramezanghorbani,David W. Romero,Alireza Moradzadeh,Olivia Viessmann,Maksim Zhdanov,John St. John,Ken Janik,David M Knigge,Yucheng Tang,Erik J Bekkers,Saee Gopal Paliwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HyenaND是一个亚二次、全局、输入依赖的算子，通过隐式参数化的全局多维卷积核直接作用于多维数据的原生几何结构，其CUDA实现nSubQ融合FFT卷积路径实现壁钟加速。在长上下文基因组学、计算机视觉、医学成像和PDE建模中，纯HyenaND堆叠匹配强注意力基线，而混合配置（交错HyenaND和注意力层）则优于纯注意力和基于递归的强混合模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意力机制的亚二次替代方法在处理多维数据时需要妥协：标准卷积缺乏全局感受野和输入依赖性，而递归模型需要将图像、体积和PDE等多维数据栅格化为违反其空间结构的特定1D扫描顺序。为解决这些问题，需要一种能直接作用于多维数据原生几何结构的亚二次、全局、输入依赖的算子。

Method: 提出HyenaND，通过隐式参数化的全局、输入依赖的多维卷积核，以亚二次复杂度直接对多维数据的原生几何结构进行卷积操作。同时开发了CUDA实现nSubQ，通过融合FFT卷积路径将HyenaND的O(L log L)缩放转化为壁钟加速。

Result: 在长上下文基因组学、计算机视觉、医学成像和PDE建模等任务中，纯HyenaND堆叠匹配了强注意力基线的准确性。而交错HyenaND和注意力层的混合配置，在性能上超越了纯注意力和基于递归的强混合模型。

Conclusion: HyenaND提供了一种有效的亚二次替代方案，能够在不牺牲全局感受野和输入依赖性的前提下直接处理多维数据，并且在多种应用场景中展现出与注意力机制相当或更优的性能。

Abstract: Subquadratic alternatives to attention require compromises when applied to multi-dimensional data: standard convolutions lack global receptive fields and input dependency, while recurrent models require rasterizing data such as images, volumes, and partial differential equation (PDE) into an ad-hoc $1\rm D$ scan order that violates their spatial structure. We introduce \textit{HyenaND}, a subquadratic, global, input-dependent operator that acts directly on the native geometry of multidimensional data through convolutions with implicitly parametrized global, input-dependent multi-dimensional convolutional kernels. Our CUDA implementation, \texttt{nSubQ}, fuses the FFT-convolution path to turn HyenaND's $\mathcal{O}(L \log L)$ scaling into wall-clock speedups. Across long-context genomics, computer vision, medical imaging, and PDE modeling, pure HyenaND stacks match the accuracy of strong attention baselines, while hybrid configurations that interleave HyenaND and attention layers outperform both pure attention and strong recurrence-based hybrids.

</details>


### [82] [Bayesian Wind Tunnels for Model Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19379)
*Siddhartha R Dalal,Vishal Misra,Abhay Parekh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究Transformer能否进行贝叶斯模型选择（识别正确的假设类别）。通过引入"贝叶斯风洞"控制实验环境，发现小模型（2.8M参数）能在纯关系性任务（如对合函数）上达到近乎最优的贝叶斯性能。但当判别统计量需要算术运算时（模加/乘），整数token成功而语义符号彻底失败，且这一边界在112倍参数缩放后仍存在。实验表明模型选择能力依赖于稳定语义的电路编译，而非整数身份本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作已证明Transformer能在固定假设类内进行精确贝叶斯滤波，但能否跨假设类进行贝叶斯模型选择尚不清楚。作者希望建立受控环境来严格检验Transformer的这一能力，并探究其边界条件。

Method: 设计了"贝叶斯风洞"——一种后验分布可闭式求解的受控环境。使用固定点自由对合（f(f(x))=x）作为纯关系性任务，以及模加/乘等需要算术运算的任务。实验在整数token和不透明符号（语义每轮变化）两种条件下进行，并进行了112倍参数规模缩放（2.8M→316M）和前沿LLM探针实验。

Result: 1) 纯关系性任务上，2.8M参数Transformer在贝叶斯最优熵差仅0.01bit；2) 非嵌套比较（对合vs三循环）类后验MAE<0.001；3) 算术任务上，整数token成功而语义符号完全失败，且112倍缩放后边界仍存；4) 稳定重标号的不透明符号可成功（0.009-bit MAE）；5) 失败定位于"头部反转+算术"组合而非头部解析本身；6) 前沿LLM定性表现贝叶斯行为但校准差距约55倍。

Conclusion: Transformer具备超越单纯贝叶斯滤波的模型选择能力，但存在关键的感知访问条件：当判别统计量需要电路化算术运算时，模型的成功依赖于稳定语义环境以编译相应电路。这一边界在极大参数缩放后仍然稳固，揭示了当前Transformer架构在符号推理上的根本性限制。

Abstract: Prior work has shown that transformers can perform exact Bayesian filtering within a fixed
  hypothesis class. Can they also perform Bayesian model selection -- identifying the correct
  hypothesis class from data? We introduce model-selection Bayesian wind tunnels: controlled
  environments where ground-truth posteriors over hypothesis classes are available in closed
  form. Using fixed-point-free involutions -- whose defining property f(f(x))=x is purely
  relational -- a 2.8M-parameter transformer achieves 0.01-bit entropy agreement with the
  Bayesian optimum (3 seeds), with both integer tokens and opaque symbols whose meanings
  change every episode. This extends to non-nested comparisons: involutions vs. 3-cycles
  (where neither class is a subset of the other) achieve class-posterior MAE under 0.001,
  demonstrating genuine model selection beyond simplicity/subset bias. We then identify a
  sharp perceptual access condition: when the discriminative statistic requires arithmetic --
  modular addition (rotations) or multiplication (f(x)=cx mod p) -- model selection succeeds
  with integer tokens but fails completely with opaque symbols, and this boundary persists
  under 112x scaling (2.8M to 316M parameters). A stationarity control confirms the operative
  factor: opaque tokens with a fixed relabeling succeed (0.009-bit MAE), showing that stable
  semantics, not integer identity, enable circuit compilation. Header subtask diagnostics
  localize the failure to the composition of header inversion with arithmetic rather than
  header parsing itself. Probing frontier LLMs on the same tasks shows qualitative Bayesian
  behavior but a large calibration gap (~55x), measured through lossy probes and therefore
  directional rather than exact.

</details>


### [83] [CruiseBench: A Real-Flight-Aligned N-CMAPSS Benchmark for Engine RUL Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19380)
*Pu Cheng,Qiang Miao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CruiseBench基准与CPM-N-CMAPSS掩码工具，通过提取巡航阶段数据解决N-CMAPSS数据量大、工况混杂问题，实现可控的RUL预测模型比较。实验显示TSMixer表现最优，消融研究证明了航段选择、时间降采样和RUL上限阈值对结果的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: N-CMAPSS数据集虽然更真实地模拟发动机退化过程，但其全航段时间序列数据量大，且退化特征与运行工况变化相互纠缠，导致预处理选择复杂，直接比较不同RUL建模方法的效果变得困难。

Method: 提出CPM-N-CMAPSS掩码工具，基于common-altitude方法识别并存储每个循环内的巡航阶段区间；在此基础上构建CruiseBench基准，对掩码后的巡航段数据施加固定协议（仅使用场景描述符与测量传感器，排除虚拟传感器、健康参数和辅助元数据，保持原始分辨率窗口，应用数据集级RUL上限），并以LSTM、GRU、TCN、TSMixer提供基线结果。

Result: 在CruiseBench-eta5-W256-S10设定下，TSMixer取得最低平均RMSE（$3.4\pm1.71$）和Saxena分数（$(2.50\pm2.99)\times 10^{4}$）。消融研究表明航段选择、时间降采样方法和RUL上限阈值均显著影响报告结果。

Conclusion: CruiseBench通过固定巡航阶段协议为可控的RUL模型比较提供了可复现的子基准，CPM-N-CMAPSS为未来的迁移学习和域适应研究提供了阶段特定的数据基础。

Abstract: Remaining useful life (RUL) prediction estimates how long an engine can continue safe operation and is central to maintenance planning. N-CMAPSS extends C-MAPSS by simulating run-to-failure aero-engine trajectories using recorded real-flight profiles and retaining complete within-flight time series rather than cycle-level snapshots. However, this added realism reduces evaluation control because full-flight records increase data volume and entangle degradation cues with operating-regime variation, complicating preprocessing choices and direct comparisons of RUL modeling performance. To mitigate this issue, this paper proposes CruiseBench, a cruise-stage RUL benchmark derived from N-CMAPSS. It introduces CPM-N-CMAPSS (Cruising-Period Mask for N-CMAPSS), a mask artifact that stores cycle-local cruising intervals identified by the common-altitude method for the nine accessible subdatasets. CruiseBench applies a fixed protocol to the masked rows, using scenario descriptors and measured sensors as inputs while excluding virtual sensors, health parameters, and auxiliary metadata from the feature tensor, preserving native-resolution windows, and applying dataset-wise RUL caps. Experiments with LSTM, GRU, TCN, and TSMixer provide baseline results for this setting. Under CruiseBench-eta5-W256-S10, TSMixer obtains the lowest average RMSE, $3.4\pm1.71$, and Saxena score, $(2.50\pm2.99)\times 10^{4}$. Ablation studies show that flight-stage selection, temporal downscaling method, and RUL-cap threshold affect reported results. With its fixed cruise-stage protocol, CruiseBench provides a reproducible sub-benchmark for controlled RUL model comparison and CPM-N-CMAPSS provides a stage-specific data foundation for future transfer-learning and domain-adaptation studies.

</details>


### [84] [Air Quality Arena: A Large-Scale Multi-Region Ground Monitoring Dataset and Benchmark for Air Quality Forecasting with Time-Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19381)
*Rishi Bharadwaj,Manik Gupta,Pandarasamy Arjunan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Air Quality Arena (AQA)，一个大规模多国家多污染物数据集与基准，涵盖7个国家4大洲6种主要污染物、超过14,000个站点-污染物序列，用于评估时间序列基础模型在空气质量短期预报中的表现。实验表明TSFMs作为零样本预测器效果显著，且最佳模型采用跨模态架构结合视觉基础模型进行时间序列预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空气污染每年导致约790万人过早死亡，亟需精准预报。现有机器学习预报基准在地理范围和污染物覆盖上过于狭窄，且未在真实大规模数据上评估最新时间序列基础模型(TSFMs)的性能。

Method: 构建AQA-Data数据集（7国4洲、6种污染物、3年跨度、14,000+序列）和AQA-Bench基准；在11个领先的TSFMs和经典基线上进行短期空气质量预报评估。

Result: TSFMs作为零样本预测器效果优异，一致优于经典基线；最佳模型采用跨模态架构，利用视觉基础模型进行时间序列预测。

Conclusion: AQA填补了空气质量预报领域大规模多国多污染物基准的空白，验证了TSFMs在该任务上的有效性，为后续研究提供了标准化评估平台。

Abstract: Air pollution causes an estimated 7.9 million premature deaths annually, making accurate forecasting a critical public health priority.
